Insights

HVAC Duct Machinery Engineering Guides

Practical, engineer‑led guides on HVAC ductwork machinery — written for buyers, fabricators and factory planners by the SBKJ Engineering team in Australia,.

Every article on this page is written and reviewed by SBKJ mechanical engineers who design, commission and service the SBAL, SBTF, RDL and TDF machine families on the shop floor. We publish what we learn from real installations across 80+ countries — so you can specify, buy and operate HVAC duct machinery with confidence. Articles are reviewed at least quarterly and updated when standards (SMACNA, EN 1505, AS/NZS 4254, DW/144) or product specifications change.

Engineer-led guide to Australian carpet + textile + broadloom + tufted + LVT + synthetic turf manufacturing HVAC: AS 1668.2 + AS 4254 + AS/NZS 60079 (latex+synthetic resin Zone 1 + polymer dust Zone 21/22 + LPG burner) + AS 3957 dust hazard (fibre + microfibre + polyester + nylon + polypropylene + wool + cotton fluff Kst 100-160) + NFPA 660 (2025) + NFPA 68/69 + NFPA 33 spray finish + NFPA 86 industrial oven. WES: respirable fibre dust 5 + inhalable 10 (baker asthma equivalent), latex SBR 0.1, styrene 50 STEL, butadiene 1 STEL, isocyanate TDI/MDI 0.005 STEL (PU TPU underlay THE KILLER), formaldehyde 1 STEL (UF MF binder IARC 1), MEK 200 + IPA 400 + acetone 250. Operators: Godfrey Hirst Carpet Group (Geelong VIC HQ + Norlane + Pakenham VIC - Mohawk Industries-owned 2018 - Australia biggest carpet), Cavalier Bremworth (NZ-owned - Tottenham VIC - ex-Feltex), Karndean Designflooring (LVT Sydney+Melbourne), Tarkett Australia LVT, Forbo Flooring linoleum, Polyflor commercial vinyl, Armstrong + Dunlop Flooring underlay, MiniJumbuk wool quilt, Interface + Shaw + Milliken + Mohawk carpet tile, TigerTurf + APT Asia Pacific Synthetic Turf + Polytan + FieldTurf + SmartGrass synthetic turf (Sydney Olympic Park + AAMI Park + school sports), Conica + Mondo polyurethane athletic track + tennis court. Industry bodies: Carpet Institute of Australia CIA + Australian Wool Innovation AWI + Floorcoverings Industry Australia FIA + International Wool Mark Company. SBKJ ductwork: GAL HVAC supply SBAL-V at GAL, 316L stainless for dye house + scouring + steam SBAL-V at 316L, spiral SBFB-1500 fibre dust + microfibre + wool + cotton + synthetic fluff + ECONYL conveying, fire-rated 250C/2hr SBSF-1525, latex + SBR + TPU + MDI/TDI spray finish booth AS/NZS 60079 Zone 1 SBAL-V at 316L.

Editorial standards

How SBKJ Engineers Write & Review These Guides

Every article on this site is held to a documented editorial process so that our readers — engineers, contractors and procurement teams — can rely on it as a primary technical reference, not as marketing copy.

Authored by working engineers

Articles are written by SBKJ mechanical engineers and field commissioning leads, not by external agencies. Each author has personally designed, built or commissioned the SBAL, SBTF, RDL or TDF machinery the article describes.

Reviewed against current standards

Standards‑related articles (SMACNA, EN 1505, AS/NZS 4254, DW/144) are checked against the latest published edition of each standard before publication. Where a standard changes, the affected article is updated and the lastReviewed date is bumped.

Quarterly review cadence

Every insight article is reviewed at least every 90 days by a senior SBKJ engineer. The reviewer's name and the review date appear at the bottom of each article so you can verify how recent the technical content is.

Plain English, real numbers

We use the same units, gauge notation and seam terminology you will see on a SMACNA, EN 1505 or AS/NZS 4254 specification — not invented marketing terms. Where we cite a number (output, tolerance, leakage class), it comes from a real SBKJ shop‑floor measurement or the relevant standard.

Corrections welcome

If you spot an error or have a question, email sales@sbkjduct.com with the article URL. Corrections are reviewed by the engineering team and applied within five working days.

No paid placement

SBKJ does not accept paid links, sponsored mentions or affiliate fees in the insights library. If we recommend a process, standard or tool, it is because we use it in our own production.
